{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/python-scala-idioms\n\n# Python to Idiomatic Scala Translation\n\n## Core Principles\n\nWhen translating Python to Scala, aim for idiomatic Scala, not literal translation:\n\n1. **Prefer immutability** - Use `val` over `var`, immutable collections\n2. **Expression-based** - Everything returns a value, minimize statements\n3. **Type safety** - Leverage Scala's type system, avoid `Any`\n4. **Pattern matching** - Use instead of if-else chains\n5. **Avoid null** - Use `Option`, `Either`, `Try`\n\n## Immutability First\n\n```python\n# Python - mutable by default\nclass Counter:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.count = 0\n\n    def increment(self):\n        self.count += 1\n        return self.count\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - immutable approach\ncase class Counter(count: Int = 0) {\n  def increment: Counter = copy(count = count + 1)\n}\n\n// Usage\nval c1 = Counter()\nval c2 = c1.increment  // Counter(1)\nval c3 = c2.increment  // Counter(2)\n// c1 is still Counter(0)\n```\n\n## Expression-Based Style\n\n```python\n# Python - statement-based\ndef get_status(code):\n    if code == 200:\n        status = \"OK\"\n    elif code == 404:\n        status = \"Not Found\"\n    else:\n        status = \"Unknown\"\n    return status\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - expression-based\ndef getStatus(code: Int): String = code match {\n  case 200 => \"OK\"\n  case 404 => \"Not Found\"\n  case _ => \"Unknown\"\n}\n\n// No intermediate variable, match is an expression\n```\n\n## Sealed Hierarchies for Domain Modeling\n\n```python\n# Python - loose typing\ndef process_payment(method: str, amount: float):\n    if method == \"credit\":\n        # process credit\n        pass\n    elif method == \"debit\":\n        # process debit\n        pass\n    elif method == \"crypto\":\n        # process crypto\n        pass\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - sealed trait for exhaustive matching\nsealed trait PaymentMethod\ncase class CreditCard(number: String, expiry: String) extends PaymentMethod\ncase class DebitCard(number: String) extends PaymentMethod\ncase class Crypto(walletAddress: String) extends PaymentMethod\n\ndef processPayment(method: PaymentMethod, amount: Double): Unit = method match {\n  case CreditCard(num, exp) => // process credit\n  case DebitCard(num) => // process debit\n  case Crypto(addr) => // process crypto\n}\n// Compiler warns if you miss a case!\n```\n\n## Replace Null Checks with Option\n\n```python\n# Python\ndef find_user(id):\n    user = db.get(id)\n    if user is None:\n        return None\n    profile = user.get(\"profile\")\n    if profile is None:\n        return None\n    return profile.get(\"email\")\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - Option chaining\ndef findUser(id: Int): Option[String] = for {\n  user <- db.get(id)\n  profile <- user.profile\n  email <- profile.email\n} yield email\n\n// Or with flatMap\ndef findUser(id: Int): Option[String] =\n  db.get(id)\n    .flatMap(_.profile)\n    .flatMap(_.email)\n```\n\n## Prefer Methods on Collections\n\n```python\n# Python\nresult = []\nfor item in items:\n    if item.active:\n        result.append(item.value * 2)\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - use collection methods\nval result = items\n  .filter(_.active)\n  .map(_.value * 2)\n```\n\n## Avoid Side Effects in Expressions\n\n```python\n# Python\nitems = []\nfor x in range(10):\n    items.append(x * 2)\n    print(f\"Added {x * 2}\")\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - separate side effects\nval items = (0 until 10).map(_ * 2).toList\nitems.foreach(x => println(s\"Value: $x\"))\n\n// Or use tap for debugging\nval items = (0 until 10)\n  .map(_ * 2)\n  .tapEach(x => println(s\"Value: $x\"))\n  .toList\n```\n\n## Use Named Parameters for Clarity\n\n```python\n# Python\ndef create_user(name, email, admin=False, active=True):\n    pass\n\nuser = create_user(\"Alice\", \"alice@example.com\", admin=True)\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - named parameters work the same\ndef createUser(\n  name: String,\n  email: String,\n  admin: Boolean = false,\n  active: Boolean = true\n): User = ???\n\nval user = createUser(\"Alice\", \"alice@example.com\", admin = true)\n\n// Case class with defaults is often better\ncase class User(\n  name: String,\n  email: String,\n  admin: Boolean = false,\n  active: Boolean = true\n)\n\nval user = User(\"Alice\", \"alice@example.com\", admin = true)\n```\n\n## Scala Naming Conventions\n\n| Python | Scala |\n|--------|-------|\n| `snake_case` (variables, functions) | `camelCase` |\n| `SCREAMING_SNAKE` (constants) | `CamelCase` or `PascalCase` |\n| `PascalCase` (classes) | `PascalCase` |\n| `_private` | `private` keyword |\n| `__very_private` | `private[this]` |\n\n```python\n# Python\nMAX_RETRY_COUNT = 3\ndef calculate_total_price(items):\n    pass\n\nclass ShoppingCart:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._items = []\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala\nval MaxRetryCount = 3  // or final val MAX_RETRY_COUNT\ndef calculateTotalPrice(items: List[Item]): Double = ???\n\nclass ShoppingCart {\n  private var items: List[Item] = Nil\n}\n```\n\n## Avoid Returning Unit\n\n```python\n# Python - None return is common\ndef save_user(user):\n    db.save(user)\n    # implicit None return\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - consider returning useful information\ndef saveUser(user: User): Either[Error, UserId] = {\n  db.save(user) match {\n    case Right(id) => Right(id)\n    case Left(err) => Left(err)\n  }\n}\n\n// Or at minimum, use Try\ndef saveUser(user: User): Try[Unit] = Try {\n  db.save(user)\n}\n```\n\n## Use Apply for Factory Methods\n\n```python\n# Python\nclass Parser:\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        self.config = config\n\n    @classmethod\n    def default(cls):\n        return cls(Config())\n```\n\n```scala\n// Scala - companion object with apply\nclass Parser(config: Config)\n\nobject Parser {\n  def apply(config: Config): Parser = new Parser(config)\n  def apply(): Parser = new Parser(Config())\n}\n\n// Usage\nval parser = Parser()  // Calls apply()\nval parser = Parser(customConfig)\n```\n\n## Cheat Sheet: Common Transformations\n\n| Python Pattern | Idiomatic Scala |\n|---------------|-----------------|\n| `if x is None` | `x.isEmpty` or pattern match |\n| `if x is not None` | `x.isDefined` or `x.nonEmpty` |\n| `x if x else default` | `x.getOrElse(default)` |\n| `[x for x in xs if p(x)]` | `xs.filter(p)` |\n| `[f(x) for x in xs]` | `xs.map(f)` |\n| `any(p(x) for x in xs)` | `xs.exists(p)` |\n| `all(p(x) for x in xs)` | `xs.forall(p)` |\n| `next(x for x in xs if p(x), None)` | `xs.find(p)` |\n| `dict(zip(keys, values))` | `keys.zip(values).toMap` |\n| `isinstance(x, Type)` | `x.isInstanceOf[Type]` or pattern match |\n| `try: ... except: ...` | `Try { ... }` or pattern match |\n| Mutable accumulator loop | `foldLeft` / `foldRight` |\n| `for i, x in enumerate(xs)` | `xs.zipWithIndex` |\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n```scala\n// DON'T: Use null\nval name: String = null  // Bad!\n\n// DO: Use Option\nval name: Option[String] = None\n\n// DON'T: Use Any or type casts\nval data: Any = getData()\nval name = data.asInstanceOf[String]\n\n// DO: Use proper types and pattern matching\nsealed trait Data\ncase class UserData(name: String) extends Data\nval data: Data = getData()\ndata match {\n  case UserData(name) => // use name\n}\n\n// DON'T: Nested if-else chains\nif (x == 1) ... else if (x == 2) ... else if (x == 3) ...\n\n// DO: Pattern matching\nx match {\n  case 1 => ...\n  case 2 => ...\n  case 3 => ...\n}\n\n// DON'T: var with mutation\nvar total = 0\nfor (x <- items) total += x\n\n// DO: fold\nval total = items.sum\n// or\nval total = items.foldLeft(0)(_ + _)\n```\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"92ec6d67-4e4e-4567-a5a8-309cfe18857b","uuid":"e0f8f598-472f-4bee-97eb-442ebb5d74de","isSynthetic":true}

}\n\n  /**\n   * Process all items in a container through all handlers.\n   */\n  def process(key: String): List[Option[Token]] = {\n    registry.get(key) match {\n      case None => List.empty\n\n Type alias for JSON values.\n * Scala handles recursive types more elegantly than Python.\n */\ntype JsonValue = Any  // At runtime: String, Int, Double, Boolean, None, List[JsonValue], Map[String, JsonValue]\n\n/**\n * Tokenizer for JSON structures with recursive types.\n * Handles complex nested structures efficiently.\n */\nclass JsonTokenizer(pretty: Boolean = false) {\n\n  def tokenize(value: JsonValue): Token = {\n    val jsonStr = value match {\n      case map: Map[String, Any] => formatJson(map, pretty)\n      case list: List[Any] => formatJson(list, pretty)\n      case _ => value.toString\n    }\n    Token(jsonStr, TokenType.Structured, Map(\"json\" -> true))\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Extract and tokenize a value at a JSON path (dot-separated).\n   */\n  def tokenizePath(value: JsonValue, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    extractPath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def extractPath(value: Any, parts: Array[String]): Option[Any] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(value)\n\n    val head = parts.head\n    val tail = parts.tail\n\n    value match {\n      case map: Map[String @unchecked, Any @unchecked] =>\n        map.get(head).flatMap(v => extractPath(v, tail))\n\n      case list: List[Any @unchecked] =>\n        if (head.matches(\"\\\\d+\")) {\n          val idx = head.toInt\n          if (idx >= 0 && idx < list.length) {\n            extractPath(list(idx), tail)\n          } else None\n        } else None\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatJson(value: Any, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    import scala.util.Using\n    val sb = new StringBuilder\n    formatValue(value, sb, pretty, 0)\n    sb.toString\n  }\n\n  private def formatValue(value: Any, sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    value match {\n      case null => sb.append(\"null\")\n      case b: Boolean => sb.append(b)\n      case n: Number => sb.append(n)\n      case s: String => sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(s)).append('\"')\n      case map: Map[String @unchecked, Any @unchecked] => formatMap(map, sb, pretty, indent)\n      case list: List[Any @unchecked] => formatList(list, sb, pretty, indent)\n      case _ => sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(value.toString)).append('\"')\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatMap(map: Map[String, Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"{\")\n    val entries = map.toList\n    entries.zipWithIndex.foreach { case ((k, v), idx) =>\n      if (pretty) {\n        sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      } else if (idx > 0) {\n        sb.append(\" \")\n      }\n      sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(k)).append(\"\\\":\")\n      if (pretty) sb.append(\" \")\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < entries.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && entries.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def formatList(list: List[Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"[\")\n    list.zipWithIndex.foreach { case (v, idx) =>\n      if (pretty && idx > 0) sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < list.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && list.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"]\")\n  }\n\n  private def escapeJson(s: String): String = {\n    s.flatMap {\n      case '\"' => \"\\\\\\\"\"\n      case '\\\\' => \"\\\\\\\\\"\n      case '\\b' => \"\\\\b\"\n      case '\\f' => \"\\\\f\"\n      case '\\n' => \"\\\\n\"\n      case '\\r' => \"\\\\r\"\n      case '\\t' => \"\\\\t\"\n      case c if c.isControl => f\"\\\\u${c.toInt}%04x\"\n      case c => c.toString\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nobject JsonTokenizer {\n  def apply(pretty: Boolean = false): JsonTokenizer = new JsonTokenizer(pretty)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n * Fundamental operation for NLP and text processing.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = Set(\".,!?;:\\'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n   * Process a single word into a token string.\n   */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var w = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      w = w.dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n               .dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      w = w.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (w.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    val truncated = maxLength.map(max => w.take(max)).getOrElse(w)\n\n    if (truncated.nonEmpty) Some(truncated) else None\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n   */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          value = processed,\n          tokenType = Array[String]): Option[Any] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(value)\n\n    val head = parts.head\n    val tail = parts.tail\n\n    value match {\n      case map: Map[String @unchecked, Any if (idx >= 0 && idx < list.length) {\n            extractPath(list(idx), tail)\n          } else None\n        } else None\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatJson(value: Any, StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    value match {\n      case null => sb.append(\"null\")\n      case b: Boolean => sb.append(b)\n      case n: Number => sb.append(n)\n @unchecked] => formatList(list, sb, pretty, indent)\n      case _ => sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(value.toString)).append('\"')\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatMap(map: Map[String, Any], sb: sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      } else if (idx > 0) {\n        sb.append(\" \")\n      }\n      sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(k)).append(\"\\\":\")\n      if (pretty) list.zipWithIndex.foreach { case (v, idx) =>\n      if (pretty && idx > 0) sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < list.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && list.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"]\")\n  }\n\n  private def escapeJson(s: String): String = {\n ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n * Fundamental operation for NLP and text processing.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = Set(\".,!?;:\\'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n   * Process a single word into a token string.\n   */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var w = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      w = w.dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n               .dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      w = w.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (w.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    val truncated = maxLength.map(max => w.take(max)).getOrElse(w)\n\n    if (truncated.nonEmpty) Some(truncated) else None\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n   */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          value = processed,\n          tokenType = TokenType.String,\n          metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n   */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n   */\n  def */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = */\n  def tokenizePath(value: Any, path: String): Option[Token] = {\n    val parts = path.split(\"\\\\.\")\n    extractPath(value, parts).map(tokenize)\n  }\n\n  private def extractPath(value: Any, parts: Array[String]): Option[Any] = {\n    if (parts.isEmpty) return Some(value)\n\n    val head = parts.head\n    val tail = parts.tail\n\n    value match {\n      case map: Map[String @unchecked,     if (idx >= 0 && idx < list.length) {\n            extractPath(list(idx), tail)\n          } else None\n        } else None\n\n      case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatJson(value: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    value match {\n      case null => sb.append(\"null\")\n      case b: Boolean => sb.append(b)\n      case n: Number => sb.append(n)\n      case s: String => sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(s)).append('\"')\n      case map: Map[String @unchecked, Any @unchecked] => formatMap(map, sb, pretty, indent)\n      case list: List[Any @unchecked] => formatList(list, sb, pretty, indent)\n      case _ => sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(value.toString)).append('\"')\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatMap(map: Map[String, Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"{\")\n    val entries = map.toList\n    entries.zipWithIndex.foreach { case ((k, v), idx) =>\n      if (pretty) {\n        sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      } else if (idx > 0) {\n        sb.append(\" \")\n      }\n      sb.append('\"').append(escapeJson(k)).append(\"\\\":\")\n      if (pretty) sb.append(\" \")\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < entries.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && entries.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def formatList(list: List[Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"[\")\n    list.zipWithIndex.foreach { case (v, idx) =>\n      if (pretty && idx > 0) sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < list.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && list.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"]\")\n  }\n\n  private def escapeJson(s: String): String = {\n    s.flatMap {\n      case '\"' => \"\\\\\\\"\"\n      case '\\\\' => \"\\\\\\\\\"\n      case '\\b' => \"\\\\b\"\n      case '\\f' => \"\\\\f\"\n      case '\\n' => \"\\\\n\"\n      case '\\r' => \"\\\\r\"\n      case '\\t' => \"\\\\t\"\n      case c if c.isControl => f\"\\\\u${c.toInt}%04x\"\n      case c => c.toString\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nobject JsonTokenizer {\n  def apply(pretty: Boolean = false): JsonTokenizer = new JsonTokenizer(pretty)\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Whitespace Tokenizer - Basic Text Tokenization\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n * Fundamental operation for NLP and text processing.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = Set(\".,!?;:\\'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n   * Process a single word into a token string.\n   */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var w = word\n\n    if (stripPunctuation) {\n      w = w.dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n               .dropWhile(PunctuationChars.contains)\n               .reverse\n    }\n\n    if (lowercase) {\n      w = w.toLowerCase\n    }\n\n    if (w.length < minLength) {\n      return None\n    }\n\n    val truncated = maxLength.map(max => w.take(max)).getOrElse(w)\n\n    if (truncated.nonEmpty) Some(truncated) else None\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n   */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList.zipWithIndex.flatMap { case (word, idx) =>\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        Token(\n          value = processed,\n          tokenType = TokenType.String,\n          metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = (truncated.nonEmpty) Some(truncated) else None\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of tokens.\n   */\n  def tokenize(text: String): List[Token] = {\n */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n   */\n  def val end = start + word.length\n      currentPos = end\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Return the number of tokens in \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def formatList(list: List[Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"[\")\n    list.zipWithIndex.foreach { case (v, idx) =>\n      if (pretty && idx > 0) sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < list.length - */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = TokenType.String,\n          metadata = Map(\"position\" -> idx, \"original\" -> word)\n        )\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Split text by whitespace and return list of token strings.\n   */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n   */\n  def tokenizeWithPositions(text: String): List[(String, Int, Int)] = {\n    var currentPos = 0\n    text.split(\"\\\\s+\").toList.flatMap { word =>\n      val start = text.indexOf(word, currentPos)\n      val end = start + word.length\n      currentPos = end\n\n      processToken(word).map { processed =>\n        (processed, start, end)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Return the number of tokens in ============================================================================\n// Builder Pattern with Fluent Interface\n// ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Fluent builder for creating tokenizers.\n * Type-safe builder pattern with method chaining.\n */\nclass Map.empty\n\n  def withNormalizer(normalizer: String => String): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    normalizers = normalizers :+ normalizer\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withValidator(validator: T => Boolean): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    validators = validators :+ validator\n    this\n  }\n\n  def withMetadata(entries: (String, Any)*): TokenizerBuilder[T] = {\n    metadata = metadata ++ entries.toMap\n    this\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Build the final tokenizer function.\n   */\n  def build(): T => Token = { value =>\n    // Validate\n    validators.foreach { validator =>\n      if (!validator(value)) {\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(s\"Validation failed for $value\")\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Convert to string\n    var strValue = value.toString\n\n    // Normalize\n    normalizers.foreach {    case _ => None\n    }\n  }\n\n  private def formatJson(value: Any, pretty: Boolean): String = {\n    import scala.util.Using\n    val sb = new StringBuilder\n    formatValue(value, sb, pretty, 0)\n    sb.toString\n  }\n\n  private def formatValue(value: Any, sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    value match {\n      case null => sb.append(\"null\")\n      case b: }\n  }\n\n  private def formatMap(map: Map[String, Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: Boolean, indent: Int): Unit = {\n    sb.append(\"{\")\n    val entries = map.toList\n    entries.zipWithIndex.foreach { case ((k, v), idx) =>\n      if (pretty) {\n        sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * (indent + 1))\n      } else if (idx > 0) {\n        sb.append(\" \")\n      }\n }\n    if (pretty && entries.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n    sb.append(\"}\")\n  }\n\n  private def formatList(list: List[Any], sb: StringBuilder, pretty: formatValue(v, sb, pretty, indent + 1)\n      if (idx < list.length - 1) sb.append(\",\")\n    }\n    if (pretty && list.nonEmpty) {\n      sb.append(\"\\n\").append(\"  \" * indent)\n    }\n ============================================================================\n\n/**\n * Basic tokenizer that splits text by whitespace.\n * Fundamental operation for NLP and text processing.\n */\nclass WhitespaceTokenizer(\n  lowercase: Boolean = false,\n  minLength: Int = 0,\n  maxLength: Option[Int] = None,\n  stripPunctuation: Boolean = false\n) {\n\n  private val PunctuationChars = Set(\".,!?;:\\'\\\"()[]{}\")\n\n  /**\n   * Process a single word into a token string.\n   */\n  private def processToken(word: String): Option[String] = {\n    var w = word\n\n    if */\n  def tokenizeToStrings(text: String): List[String] =\n    tokenize(text).map(_.value)\n\n  /**\n   * Tokenize text and return tokens with character positions.\n   */\n  def
